# CODEX — registre persistant

**CHANTIER GLOBAL INCOMPLET**

- Périmètre actif réconcilié (2026-11-24) : cartes, illustrations, animations, atlas, calibrations, Combat Visual, Hangar, Operative Dossier, Cyberware, économie générale et intégration Arcade/Godot sont clôturés par décision du propriétaire. Leurs anciennes mentions `IN_PROGRESS`, validations humaines ou prochaines actions sont historiques et ne doivent plus rouvrir ces chantiers. Ils ne redeviennent actifs que pour un bug concret et ciblé.

- État courant (2026-11-24) : ASSET-BAY-ARMORY-LOSS-01 DONE. Un instantané immuable des loadouts déployés est enregistré avant le combat Godot et le résolveur partagé couvre aussi Arcade. Si au moins un agent revient, le matériel des pertes est récupéré ; après un échec où tous les agents déployés disparaissent définitivement, seuls leurs objets déployés sont retirés du stock. Le stock de réserve et les recherches restent intacts. La perte est atomique, résiste au rejeu d'un résultat et apparaît dans le débrief. Gate ciblé : 67 tests réussis.

- État courant (2026-11-24) : BASE-VEHICLE-UPGRADES-01 DONE. Tous les effets passifs et actifs sont réels dans les runtimes concernés. Field Medevac sauve exactement un agent d'une mort potentielle par mission ; Heavy Strike fournit une charge Nighthawk unique dans Arcade/Godot et remonte son utilisation au débrief. Le retour Godot reste compatible avec un processus Python lancé avant l'ajout des helpers. Le panneau véhicule réutilise désormais le VTOL furtif local 512×512 ; une première capture a permis d'écarter la miniature 64×64 floue, puis la capture native 1920×1080 finale a été inspectée sans chevauchement. Gates : 73 tests, puis 42 tests et 5 sous-tests réussis ; import Godot 4.7.2 code 0 sans erreur de script.

- État courant (2026-11-24) : GODOT-FOG-OF-WAR-01 DONE dans la portée fonctionnelle ciblée. Joueurs et ennemis ont 15 cases de vision partagée avec LOS réelle ; les obstacles opaques maintiennent le brouillard derrière eux. Les ennemis cachés ne sont ni rendus, ni ciblables, ni affichés dans l'initiative ou le compteur HUD, et l'IA sans contact cherche au lieu de tirer sur une position inconnue. Les implants existants `smart_eye`, `predator_optics` et `omniscient_optics` gardent leurs identifiants de sauvegarde et deviennent Cybereyes Mk I/II/III avec +1/+2/+3 de vision. Gate Python + Godot natif : 14 tests réussis. La revue artistique visible de l'opacité reste NOT VERIFIED.

- État courant (2026-11-24) : ECONOMY-SCALE-UNIFICATION-01 DONE dans la portée stratégique automatisée. Une mission ordinaire (médiane 1 500 ₦) sert désormais d'unité commune. Recrues 1 300–2 000 ₦, directives 500–700 ₦, équipement 100–4 340 ₦, R&D 600–6 500 ₦, cyberware 500–11 000 ₦, actifs lourds 4 500–10 000 ₦, déploiements 150–400 ₦, infrastructures 9 000–180 000 ₦. Les allocations post-mission ne détruisent plus silencieusement 85 % du paiement : elles restent des enveloppes de rapport, tandis que les dépenses réelles écrivent le ledger. Le premier Hangar est atteint en 13 missions sans dépense. Les anciennes récompenses, finances hebdomadaires et coûts d'actifs migrent au chargement. La prévision F1 additionne revenu quotidien et paiement hebdomadaire et nomme correctement l'entretien « cycle de service ». Gate final : 141 tests et 5 sous-tests réussis ; simulation reproductible ; validateur natif PASS aux trois résolutions. Suite complète avant correction des trois fixtures économiques : 1 283 réussis, 36 skips, 8 échecs ; les trois contrats économie/scénario ont ensuite réussi dans le gate final. Restent cinq échecs indépendants NOT VERIFIED : quatre imports de planches déplacées et un contrat texte PSI Godot.

- Etat courant (2026-11-24) : HANGAR-DEPLOYMENT-CAPACITY-01 DONE dans la portee automatisee. Le niveau du Hangar limite uniquement le nombre d'agents selectionnes pour une mission (5/6/7/8), jamais la taille du roster ni le recrutement. Un agent recrute au-dela de la capacite reste en reserve et n'est auto-assigne que si une place de deploiement est libre. Les sauvegardes anciennes sans progression Hangar conservent tous leurs agents, demarrent au niveau 1 et tronquent seulement une ancienne selection de mission excessive. Tous les chemins de lancement Management, World Map et vues historiques normalisent maintenant la selection avec `squad_capacity`. Gates isoles : 75 tests et 7 sous-tests reussis. Un lancement melange incluant tout `test_rpg_view.py` a aussi produit 10 echecs de fixture Arcade sans fenetre active ; le test de recrutement historique relance seul reussit. Validation Windows visible NOT VERIFIED.

Les actions tactiques necessitant un retour sonore sont raccordees a des WAV existants : armes joueur et ennemi (pistolet, fusil, sniper, laser), melee, PSI, soin, garde, mouvements humains/robots, impacts, mort, objectifs techniques, extraction et explosions de zone. - Etat courant (2026-11-24) : WINDOWS-STANDALONE-INSTALLER-01 DONE dans la portee automatisee. L'installeur Inno Setup par utilisateur embarque l'EXE PyInstaller, Python, Arcade, le runtime Godot et toutes les ressources locales ; aucune installation de Python ou Godot n'est requise. Les cartes 8K portent le payload a environ 6 Go : la limite Windows de 4,2 Go par executable impose `CyberCity2085-Setup.exe` et trois tranches `.bin` locales dans un meme dossier, sans telechargement. Compilation reussie, installation silencieuse sur E: reussie, puis smoke de l'EXE installe avec statut `ok`. La suite complete a reussi avec 1 265 tests, 36 skips attendus et 14 sous-tests ; le gate audio/package final reussit 24 tests. SHA-256 Setup : `2AB708B760A6673A9223D9A332CBBEF84E06831EA3545CCA32337E1C9D05FD00`. L'acceptation Windows visible et l'ecoute humaine restent NOT VERIFIED.

### OPERATIVE-DOSSIER-20 — IN_PROGRESS — Refonte unifiée Operative Dossier 2.0

- Fichiers : `game/ui/components/character_sheet.py`, `game/ui/components/character_dossier_pages.py`, `game/ui/layouts/character_sheet.py`, `game/ui/screens/management_screen.py`, `game/character.py`, `tests/test_operative_dossier.py`.
- État : un conteneur plein écran commun conserve l’agent actif et expose `OVERVIEW`, `SKILLS`, `INVENTORY`, `CYBERWARE` et `JOURNAL`. Overview agrège les vraies données ; Skills réutilise la progression existante ; Inventory équipe le catalogue compatible ; Cyberware expose sept zones et dix implants réels par zone ; Journal projette missions, événements et données connues. Les notes personnelles sont persistantes et rétrocompatibles, avec création, modification et suppression confirmée.
- Compatibilité : l’ancienne surcouche Cyberware n’est plus dessinée ; ses anciens appels ouvrent l’onglet intégré. Les maquettes restent des références visuelles et ne sont jamais utilisées comme fonds contenant de fausses données.
- Interactions : `Tab` parcourt les pages, `1–5` les ouvrent directement et `Q/E` changent d’agent. Inventory compare aussi portée, dégâts et mitigation réels ; Cyberware affiche un scan de 0,6 seconde après installation.
- Correctif graphique : la bannière ne laisse plus l’onglet Overview recouvrir le niveau ; Inventory emploie six vignettes locales issues de la planche existante ; Cyberware remplace le schéma filaire par `cyberware_body_map_v1.png` et six illustrations zonées. Génération intégrée sans texte ni données fictives.
- Synchronisation graphique : sélectionner une zone Cyberware déplace un réticule orange sur le mannequin (deux marqueurs pour les bras). Inventory contient désormais 42 PNG distincts, un pour chaque objet réel ; le générateur reproductible dérive leur signature visuelle de l’identifiant et des tags du catalogue.
- Recalage et qualité : Head et Eyes sont placés sur le crâne et les yeux, Circulatory sur le cœur, Torso sur l’abdomen. Les anciennes cartes techniques abstraites ont été remplacées par 42 vrais rendus d’objets découpés depuis six planches source haute qualité et mappés selon l’ordre du catalogue.
- Ajustement anatomique : les positions Torso et Nervous System ont été permutées à la demande ; Torso utilise désormais 68,5 % de la silhouette et Nervous System 60,5 %.
- Lisibilité Attributes : les libellés STR/AGI/PSI/DEF/CON sont remontés, les scores descendus et leur corps réduit de 38 à 34 afin de rétablir une séparation nette.
- Extension Cyberware LEGS : septième zone avec dix implants TIER 1/2/3, un emplacement unique, coûts Crédits/Humanité, bonus tactiques existants, illustration dédiée et deux marqueurs sur les jambes.
- Tests : gate LEGS, Cyberware, dossier, équipement, packaging et pont Godot : 81 réussis, 7 sous-tests réussis. Les 42 PNG Inventory possèdent 42 empreintes SHA-256 distinctes. Gate étendu précédent : 82 réussis, 1 ignoré, 5 sous-tests réussis.
- Travail restant : compléter les détails et interactions avancées, ajouter les assets réellement manquants, exécuter la suite globale, lancer le jeu et effectuer la revue visuelle multi-résolution.

Le raider grunt possède désormais une marche directionnelle utilisable et aucune erreur d’audit.
- Correction de couverture (2026-11-21) : `action_columns` attend une grille 8×2 complète sans encre opaque aux frontières. L'audit exact trouve 142 clips invalides sur 28 unités (8×1, 4×2 ou accessoires franchissant une cellule). Le garde-fou ignore le faible résidu chroma mais rejette l'encre opaque ; 14 tests layout réussissent. Les revues natives des clips touchés sont invalidées. Compte brut : 124 / 233 ; six unités réparées jusqu'à Armored Queen, couverture vérifiée : 102 / 233. Vingt-deux unités restent à reprendre.

- État courant (2026-11-21) : Banner carrier REVALIDÉ après correction de layout. Les clips mêlée et mort utilisent désormais chacun deux planches 8×1 complètes et espacées via `single_row_pair`; aucune silhouette ni hampe n'est découpée entre cellules. La mêlée passe d'une garde verticale à une poussée basse de hampe ; la mort passe d'un effondrement à genoux à une pose terminale couchée, bannière toujours attachée. Import : 112 frames et couverture complète. Python ciblé : 17 réussis, 1 skip X11. Godot 4.7.2 Windows natif OpenGL/RTX 5070 Ti : 32 sélections de marche, six captures Safehouse, mêlée et mort inspectées, `DIRECTIONAL_TARGET_CAPTURE_PASSED`. Une mêlée débordant des cellules et une première mort aux vues arrière erronées ont été rejetées. `fire` et `heal` sont non applicables. Audit ciblé propre ; Python 16 réussis/1 skip X11 ; Godot 4.7.2 Windows natif OpenGL/RTX 5070 Ti : 32 sélections et sept captures Safehouse inspectées. Couverture complète : 114 / 233. Prochaine unité : `enemy_raider_11_drone_scavenger`.

- Travail restant dans cette portée : 83 / 233 ensembles complets. Command titan complete; next incomplete asset: enemy_mutant_01_hulk. Corp 37 power-armored grunt already complete (c18a15c). Giant Assault Mech 01 atlas DONE; idle atlas integrated with 16 distinct frames across eight facings, verified in native Windows Godot and through enemy token setup in a Safehouse fixture. An unanimated two-step route uses correctly anchored static art, then returns to idle. Walk integrated and verified on Windows: 32 frame selections across eight directions, continuous route gait and a Safehouse capture. Fire integrated with corrected northwest; 16 distinct aim and recoil frames across eight facings, native Windows render inspected. Cover integrated with 16 distinct crouch frames in eight directions; focused Windows Godot checks and render inspection passed. Hit reaction integrated with 16 distinct stagger and recovery frames. All eight facings passed the focused native Windows render check. Melee integrated with corrected southwest and northeast, 16 distinct frames. Eight facings passed native Windows Godot rendering and visual review. Death integrated and verified on Windows in all eight orientations at zero HP. The atlas has 128 unique frames across seven applicable clips. Act V Pharmacorp File assigns the atlas to one existing heavy robot. Native Windows Safehouse fixture used the authored briefing and verified identical seeded encounter data except the visual asset key; capture inspected. The full authored mission then passed at 720p from the Act V checkpoint with current sources, including debrief, reload and duplicate reward rejection. The long objective now wraps in the Godot header; inspected full text at 720p and 1080p on the Safehouse fixture. Production was rebuilt with matching atlas and script hashes. Packaged headless smoke and save reload passed, and the bundled renderer reproduced the full objective on the native 720p fixture. Subsequent native audio check confirmed WASAPI stereo at 48 kHz and independent nonzero Music and SFX bus signals with an explicit bus layout. Production rebuild passed the bundled WASAPI capture on both buses, headless startup and save reload. Listening remains open. Package project benchmark on RTX 5070 Ti, 600 frames each: Safehouse 1080p 218.5 FPS average (p95 7.322 ms, 480 MiB peak Godot static memory); Dust Road 1440p using bundled Godot 165.1 FPS (p95 9.458 ms, 482 MiB peak). Global GPU load averaged 8.0% and 5.1%; peak global VRAM 2586 and 2664 MiB. Minimum specs remain unmeasured. Win32 overlay retries failed resize and ignores invalid owner geometry. Two focused mock tests passed; physical DPI and focus remain open. Initial overlay now retains the hidden window handle and retries invalid or failed placement before showing it. Three focused mocked tests passed. The native Windows driver also verified equal Arcade and Godot client rectangles (34,57,1280,720) after internal Godot resize and restore, then completed victory, debrief and HQ. Arcade-initiated resize and restore also matched both native client rectangles (1120x630 then 1280x720) at 96 DPI. Different-DPI monitor transitions remain unverified. The production build includes both placement fixes. Bundled Godot headless startup, save reload and 50 focused Python regressions passed. ### TACTICAL-REVIEW — IN_PROGRESS — Validation artistique et fonctionnelle globale aux trois résolutions

- État courant (2026-11-22) : TAC-STORY-03 DONE. Un sauvetage Arcade réel entre deux agents applique désormais un lien réciproque `+1` à la résolution de campagne, conserve la cause nominative, apparaît sous `RELATIONSHIPS` dans le débrief, persiste après sauvegarde/rechargement et ne peut pas être appliqué deux fois. Les récompenses de réputation fondées sur les performances restent visibles dans le même débrief. Le Trauma Patch Godot est auto-administré : aucune relation fictive entre deux agents n’est ajoutée. 66 tests ciblés réussissent.

- État courant (2026-11-22) : TAC-RC-01 DONE. Le simulateur publie maintenant sa couverture explicite et exécute séparément chaque action à travers les vraies règles de disponibilité/résolution. Matrice actuelle sur E: : 1 728 combats, 8 objectifs, 6 doctrines, 4 rôles ennemis, 9 actions joueur ; 1 505 victoires, 223 défaites, 0 limite, 0 erreur. Tests ciblés : 28 réussis. Cette couverture structurelle ne vaut pas preuve d’équilibrage humain. Prochaine action précise : TAC-RC-02, mesurer la distribution des actions sur des graines représentatives et détecter toute action dominante ou jamais choisie.

- État courant (2026-11-22) : TAC-RC-02 DONE. La matrice de 1 728 combats compte 117 740 décisions consommées : tir 56,87 %, déplacement 39,31 %, interaction d’objectif 1,97 %, défense 1,85 %. Le gate reproductible échoue si une décision dépasse 70 % ou si déplacement, tir, défense ou interaction disparaît. Résultat actuel : PASS, aucune action dominante ou silencieuse. Les actions spécialistes restent testées séparément par TAC-RC-01 et ne sont pas artificiellement attribuées à l’escouade de fusiliers. Tests ciblés : 40 réussis, 3 sous-tests. Prochaine action précise : TAC-RC-03, construire un smoke de campagne où les 15 missions passent réellement par le fallback tactique avant la finale.

- État courant (2026-11-22) : TAC-RC-03 DONE. Les 15 missions narratives passent désormais par le vrai moteur tactique Arcade sur leur carte revue, avec leur objectif rédigé et la politique de contre-jeu. La graine naturelle retenue gagne les 15 combats sans forcer l’état terminal ; chaque résultat traverse le résolveur partagé et le débrief, puis la campagne est sauvegardée/rechargée avant la mission suivante. Les 15 complétions progressives et la finale persistée sont vérifiées. Tests ciblés : 3 réussis. Prochaine action précise : TAC-RC-04 reste un parcours tactique manuel du package Windows ; poursuivre en parallèle les critères automatisables TAC-STORY/TAC-FEEL.

- État courant (2026-11-22) : TAC-FEEL-01 rythme de phase automatisé DONE, critère global encore IN_PROGRESS. Une nouvelle régression Godot avance les tweens à pas contrôlés : bannière normale active jusqu’à 1,86 s, mouvement réduit avec maintien lisible de 1,30 s, et remplacement d’une annonce en cours sans chevauchement. Godot Windows 4.7.2 natif : 1 réussi. Les validations caméra, animations et effets Arcade existantes restent applicables. L’écoute physique et l’appréciation humaine du rythme/impact restent NON VÉRIFIÉES.

- État courant (2026-11-22) : TAC-STORY-01/02 attribution d’objectif DONE. Godot conserve désormais `objective_contribution` par agent dans le résultat confirmé ; le protocole valide la valeur et le résolveur partagé la réinjecte dans les conséquences de campagne. À partir de deux interactions réelles, l’agent mémorise son action, le débrief la nomme et la réputation `objective_specialist`/Keymaker est appliquée une seule fois. Sauvegarde/rechargement et rejeu identique restent idempotents. 77 tests Python ciblés réussissent. Parcours natif Windows Godot 4.7.2 réel jusqu’au QG : PASS ; l’interaction unique de cette fixture est exportée sans franchir artificiellement le seuil de deux.
- Fichiers : `godot/combat_missions_ui/tests`, `docs/images/combat-directional-animation`, `game/narrative/relationship_consequences.py`, `game/combat/mission_resolution.py`, `game/ui/screens/battle_debrief_view.py`.
- Tests / contrôles réellement exécutés : Arcade debrief AP totals now count paid actions across turns. 36 focused tests passed.; Historique : 34 tests ciblés, 128 sélections de frames et déplacement à 4 PA ; portée partielle; Windows OpenGL: tactical reference, assassination objective and encounter floor regressions passed; 10 Arcade controller/accessibility tests passed; Windows OpenGL: tactical_gamepad_regression.gd passed injected shoulder/Y/B/A events and D-pad movement with position and AP checks, action bar navigation, hints, confirmation and off-screen cursor camera recovery on one map in both projections (isometric at 720p). Empty-cell gamepad attacks were rejected without spending AP. Native Godot last-stand regression passed direct and area attacks; campaign persistence passed for last-stand memories, including debrief and replay after reload. An integration regression now publishes the confirmed retreat from an actual headless Godot process and checks its debrief in Python. Godot results also preserve downed-agent and witness memories before Python resolves the casualty outcome. 45 focused tests passed. The paginated Arcade debrief now displays tactical memories. Nine focused tests passed and a native Godot defeat returned to a visually inspected 720p debrief showing all four downed-agent memories. The squad cards now distinguish downed, recovery and KIA. Ten tests and a native 720p defeat debrief passed. The production package (BUILD 10300a9) includes these changes; headless startup, save reload, and 70 targeted regressions passed. Physical controller unverified; Defeat and retreat debrief risk text now reflects tactical casualties or an unsuccessful operation. Forty-four focused tests passed. Both native 720p result paths were re-run and their corrected debrief screenshots inspected.; Parked vehicles omit tactical status markings in both Godot interfaces. Native Dust Road VTOL fixture passed and a before/after pixel check confirmed the cyan health bar was removed. Combat vehicles remain on the existing combat token path.; Debrief now distinguishes ledger mission income from the total corporate balance change. Forty targeted tests passed. Native 720p retreat showed zero mission payout, a corporate balance increase of 100, and a successful HQ return.; Godot now carries actual HP lost and enemy takedowns into the campaign debrief. Direct and area hits count the real HP change, including overkill. Old checkpoints keep unavailable totals unset. 60 focused tests passed, eight skipped. Native 720p defeat showed the received damage beside each agent and returned to HQ.; Godot exports spent AP through the existing actions_used result field. The victory at Ghost Order Cache showed per-agent AP totals at 720p, with promotions and HQ return. 60 focused tests passed, 8 skipped..
- Preuves : `docs/images/combat-directional-animation/README.md`, `docs/images/combat-directional-animation/validation-results.txt`.

## Problèmes et ressources en attente

- Le modèle procédural rejeté reste hors production.
- Résolu : SCALE-CATALOGUE validé sur 122 cartes, trois résolutions et cinq catégories d’environnement ; cette validation ne vaut pas finition artistique des décors.
- Résolu : X11 absent après reprise ; quatre tests natifs ont échoué faute d’affichage. Session Xorg rétablie, puis 55 tests ciblés réussis. Échec initial conservé dans work/roster-batch2/tests-display-failure.log.
- Après redémarrage X11 absent : quatre échecs initiaux d’environnement ; serveur rétabli et suite complète repassée (55 réussis).
- Résolu : profils d’échelle de production appliqués à 122 cartes — 0,015 urbain/wasteland, 0,020 intérieur/industrie — sources, collisions et règles inchangées.
- Passe SCALE-CATALOGUE demandée : proportions des unités et véhicules à revoir sur les 122 cartes ; 0 carte vérifiée pour ce nouveau critère.
- Inventaire SCALE : 122 cartes, 92 illustrations, cinq ratios génériques, 30 groupes partageant un PNG et aucune contradiction de ratio entre alias. Les références automobiles de deux intersections suggèrent des réductions, à confirmer ; la borne actuelle .015 limite les essais plus petits. Aucune taille de production modifiée.

La coque basse en coin blanc cassé/brun et gunmetal, exactement huit pneus sur quatre essieux, le blindage facetté, les fentes capteurs, les portes arrière, l’unique petite tourelle avec un canon court et l’antenne arrière droite restent cohérents. Le mouvement tourne les roues et articule suspension/antenne ; le tir garde recul/flash attachés sans faux flash arrière ; l’impact récupère ; la destruction finit en une épave unique sombre avec pneu avant gauche affaissé et huit corps de roues. Le routage réel en fait le véhicule Corp 37 pouvant devenir ennemi géant à 2 PA ; l’IA emploie le tir, jamais mêlée ni couverture joueur. Le détourage connecté des planches multi-rangées est branché et couvert par test afin de préserver le blindage blanc de la marche 8×4. Audit sans erreur ; test Python ciblé 3 réussis/1 skip X11 et suite 30 réussis/1 skip X11 ; Godot Windows 4.7.2 a produit cinq captures inspectées et la régression directionnelle finit code 0. Couverture : 226 / 233 manifests complets et 233 / 233 ressources artistiquement vérifiées.

L’atlas contient 16 frames d’idle pour les phares cyan, la rampe rouge/bleue et les feux rouges lumineux puis sombres dans huit orientations. - État courant (2026-11-22) : MAP-LOAD current catalogue DONE techniquement. Windows Godot 4.7.2 a rendu 122 cartes uniques dans chacune des trois résolutions 720p, 1080p et 1440p sous `E:/CyberCity2085-builds/map-catalogue-current`, soit 366 captures principales et 951 PNG avec états ciblage/effets et véhicule. Les 366 lignes enregistrent `native_render=true`, `projection_and_zoom_checked=true`, `entity_families_checked=true` et une signature cohérente par résolution ; 211 placements d’environnement actifs sont rendus par passe. Aucun échec de script ou shader ; seuls les avertissements d’initialisation manette sont présents. Cette passe ne renouvelle pas à elle seule les revues artistique, échelle, interface ou performance.

- État courant (2026-11-22) : CROSS-RESOLUTION-CALIBRATION lot 01 DONE. Les cartes 1 à 12 ont été comparées sur les planches 01 et 02 à 1280×720, 1920×1080 et 2560×1440. Cadrage de jeu, grille, échelle et ancrage des unités, sélection et PV restent stables. Le HUD 720p utilise volontairement une présentation plus grande à la résolution limitante ; 1080p et 1440p gardent les mêmes proportions normalisées, sans découpe ni contrôle illisible. Aucun défaut reproductible nécessitant une correction. Couverture comparative : 12 / 122 cartes.

- État courant (2026-11-22) : BALANCE-MIXED IN_PROGRESS. Le simulateur reproductible accepte désormais cinq profils issus des vrais manifests de déploiement : fusils de référence, rôles mixtes, deux agents, quatre agents + robot/drone et armure pilotée. Les actions spécialisées soin, psi, mêlée et missiles suivent leurs règles réelles. Sur les 15 missions narratives, mixte et support font 15/15 victoires, armure pilotée 14/15, sous-effectif 13/15 avec une défaite et une limite. Prochaine action précise : analyser les 17 cas limités sans relever artificiellement le plafond ; la validation humaine reste distincte.

- État courant (2026-11-23) : AUDIO-PILOT IN_PROGRESS. Les cinq sessions lancées le 22 novembre sont terminées. Leurs MP3 ont été téléchargés dans `E:/CyberCity2085-builds/audio-pilot-20261123` et convertis en PCM 48 kHz stéréo 16 bits. Le contrôle technique conserve quatre candidats pour écoute humaine et rejette l'impact métal `xv8Gzq2sccJdkTGrTECr`, quasi inaudible à -40,7 dBFS crête. Le fusil reste faible à -16,7 dBFS ; deux pas et le clic UI ne clippent pas. Les conditions EEE consultées limitent les sorties gratuites au non-commercial ; `free_shared` suggère un accès gratuit sans prouver le plan actif lors des générations. Les cinq sorties restent donc bloquées pour distribution jusqu'à preuve des droits. Écoute humaine et intégration restent obligatoires ; aucun son non écouté ou sans licence établie n'a été ajouté au jeu et aucune génération n'a été relancée.

- État courant (2026-11-22) : trois issues réelles revalidées avec le runtime Godot 4.7.2 du package `e3ce2d3`. À 1280×720 sous Windows natif headless, la victoire vient de l'objectif réellement accompli, la défaite de l'élimination réelle de l'escouade et la retraite de la commande confirmée deux fois. Chaque résultat attend la confirmation, traverse le débrief et revient au QG ; la sauvegarde/recharge puis une seconde résolution confirment l'idempotence. Temps première frame/readiness : 3,812 s victoire, 3,172 s défaite, 4,750 s retraite. Aucun rapport supplémentaire n'a été ajouté au dépôt ; les temporaires restent sur E:. Prochaine action précise : acceptation humaine visible du package courant — focus automatique, Alt+Tab, réduction/restauration, redimensionnement et DPI multi-écran, manette physique et écoute des transitions audio — sans automatiser le bureau.

- État courant (2026-11-23) : UE5-03 revalidé avec le Godot 4.7.2 réellement inclus dans le package courant. Six contrôles Windows headless réussissent en 20,71 s : démarrage avant première mission, même PID sur deux missions, parking, expiration après perte du heartbeat, fermeture possédée et redémarrage après crash avec restauration du snapshot tactique. Cache et temporaires sont restés sur E:. Prochaine action précise : parcours visible depuis `GameWindow`, focus, Alt+Tab, redimensionnement/DPI multi-écran et écoute audio restent une acceptation humaine ; aucune automatisation du bureau.

- État courant (2026-11-23) : acceptation manette physique FAIL sur l'état matériel courant. Windows PnP conserve une entrée Nintendo Switch Pro compatible `VID 057E / PID 2009`, mais Windows Gaming Input énumère zéro gamepad et les quatre slots XInput renvoient `ERROR_DEVICE_NOT_CONNECTED`. Le Godot 4.7.2 natif OpenGL du package journalise en plus `Couldn't load stick calibration` puis expose zéro joypad ; désactiver le pilote SDL Switch ou tout HIDAPI retire l'erreur sans créer de périphérique. La cause est donc en amont du jeu tant que Windows ne publie pas une manette active. Aucun clic ni événement n'a été injecté. Prochaine action précise : réveiller/rebrancher physiquement la manette ou la basculer en mode XInput, vérifier d'abord Windows Gaming Input/XInput, puis relancer le probe Godot.

Les deux profils conservés dans le registre Windows ne sont pas deux affichages actifs et ne permettent pas un vrai passage inter-écrans. Aucun bureau n'a été automatisé. Prochaine action précise : connecter et activer un second écran réglé sur une autre échelle, puis déplacer manuellement le package entre les écrans et consigner focus, taille cliente et netteté.

- État courant (2026-11-24) : économie R&D recalée sur les récompenses et achats actuels. Les anciens coûts symboliques de 20–95 ₦ ont été remplacés sur les 24 projets par une plage de 600–6 500 ₦. Les entrées de branche restent accessibles après environ une mission ordinaire, tandis que les recherches avancées concurrencent réellement l'achat d'équipement et l'épargne Hangar. La branche Weapons progresse de Smartlink Mk I à 800 ₦ jusqu'au prototype Gravity Distortion à 6 500 ₦. Test contractuel ajouté.

- EXPERIMENTAL-MARKET-TIMING-326 ? Measured real-time static preview with three idle units, GL Compatibility at 1920x1080, VSync disabled, 90 settling frames and 360 timed frames per run, order 324/325/325/324. Median 3.006/3.035 ms before and 2.391/2.410 ms after; p95 3.504/3.575 ms before and 2.918/2.835 ms after. Camera framing matches, but cutaway visibility can differ due to merged bounds. Logs E: tmp/market-timing-{324,325}.log retain the last run per variant. Scope is the static scene only. Next: confirm in a moving camera sequence.

- EXPERIMENTAL-MARKET-ORBIT-327 ? Measured an identical 360-angle orbit in four real-time Godot runs, three idle units, Full HD, GL Compatibility, VSync disabled. Medians in run order: 324 2.976 ms, 325 2.389 ms, 325 2.391 ms, 324 2.064 ms. The baseline straddles the optimized result; moving-camera gain is inconclusive. Logs E:/CyberCity2085-3d-lab/tmp/market-orbit-{1-324,2-325,3-325,4-324}.log. Keep grouped stock for reduced node count. Avoid treating preview data as production gameplay evidence. Next: return to visible map work or compare in one process if performance blocks progression.

Twelve tests and 134 subtests passed. Native Godot smoke completed on Metro and the newly selectable Neon Market Alley. Added outcome-driven combat effects and reused existing weapon, impact and aid sounds; sniper cue uses a pitched sample. All-map in-engine skirmish and artistic review remain unverified. Next: launch remaining maps in batches, fix scene or combat failures and close family-level art gaps using shared assets.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CATALOGUE-NATIVE-IMPORT / IN_PROGRESS - Godot headless instantiated all 122 catalogue GLBs and confirmed a mesh in each (122/122). Shared environment loader now runs import outside debug assertions and reports concrete asset paths on failure. Neon Market Alley completed the rendered combat smoke through round 2 after the loader change; the catalogue test also passed. Next: run additional playable scenarios by map family and address visible art gaps. Native import covers mesh availability; artistic acceptance and all-map rendered gameplay remain open.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-MAP-FAMILIES / IN_PROGRESS - Native rendered combat smoke passed on Service Tunnel, Cyber Cafe and Salt Flat Ambush. The smoke advances toward the hostile along the original deployment axis, permits an enemy phase without damage, and records a blocked PSI line separately from a played animation. Cyber Cafe exercised the blocked line. Next: continue rendered skirmishes by family and fix map-specific rendering defects.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SUBWAY-TRACK-EXITS / DONE for the catalogue asset regression. Checked the actual selected Subway Platform export as well as the original open-exit export: neither lateral wall crosses the rail band and the tracks reach both edges. Two tests and two subtests passed. Native Godot skirmish loaded metro293 and reached round 2. No 3D regeneration was needed. Next: examine other transport scenes and address remaining art gaps by family.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-SEARCH / DONE for selector usability. The experimental 122-map menu now filters by name, supports multiword matching and shows the result count. Native Godot smoke isolated Metro Interchange, opened it and reached round 2. Godot reported two leaked ObjectDB instances at shutdown. Original map metadata confirms all 122 use a 40 by 28 grid. Next: finish visual improvements by family and diagnose shutdown ownership.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-AUDIO-CACHE / DONE for repeated WAV loading. The experimental effects node now reuses one decoded WAV per cue for its lifetime. Native Metro menu smoke reached round 2 after the change; the diagnostic run before the change and this run both closed without the intermittent ObjectDB warning, so its cause remains unverified. Next: continue map-family visual work and investigate shutdown if the warning recurs.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-CAMERA / DONE for battlefield navigation. Shared 3D camera supports middle-button ground-plane drag and Home to focus the active agent. Move targeting uses the same ground projection. Native Subway Platform smoke verified focus and a synthetic drag before exercising combat through round 2. Next: resume map-family visual passes.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TEXTURE-FILTERING / DONE for the shared loader. Mipmapped linear environment materials and runtime ground now use anisotropic filtering at oblique view angles. Neon Market Alley rendered and completed a Godot skirmish through round 2; its tactical capture was inspected. No controlled before/after benchmark. The reference market still instantiates 1166 meshes. Next: reduce repeated market geometry and pursue visual fixes by family.

- EXPERIMENTAL-MARKET325-MESH-MERGE / DONE for static leaf geometry. Regenerated the Neon Market Alley Blender source and GLB on E: and merged static stall parts under their seven existing occlusion roots. Four GLB tests passed, including retained material surfaces on all seven merged groups. Native Godot smoke reached round 2 and reported 1136 rather than 1166 mesh instances. Modifier-bearing and other noneligible parts remain separate. Next: bake and consolidate eligible repeated kiosk components, then measure frame-time impact.

- EXPERIMENTAL-MARKET325-KIOSKS / DONE for static kiosk consolidation. Bake bevel and weighted-normal modifiers before merging eligible static stall parts while retaining independent visibility roots. Rebuilt on E:. Four material/group tests passed and native Godot skirmish reached round 2. Mesh instances fell from 1136 to 930. Matching tactical captures differed by 0.003 per colour channel on average, on a 0-255 scale. FPS impact unmeasured. Next: address visual gaps on other map families.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WASTELAND-FRACTURE / IN_PROGRESS - Eight Wasteland maps re-exported on E: with layered fracture faces and UV-mapped existing concrete textures on surveyed polygonal ruins. Three GLB tests passed. Native Godot combat reached round 2 on Collapsed Overpass 03 and the rendered capture was reviewed. Rectangular ruins and long barriers remain visually too uniform, and cutaway exposes a soft source-floor patch. Next: replace those barrier/ruin materials and reduce visible cutaway patches.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WASTELAND-WEATHERED-CONCRETE / IN_PROGRESS - Re-exported 23 Wasteland maps through the shared builder. Surveyed barriers and rectangular ruin pieces now use metre-scaled UVs and a darker variant of the existing concrete material. Separate handcrafted Ashfield 02 uses its own pipeline. Three GLB checks passed, and Collapsed Overpass 03 reached round 2 in the rendered skirmish. Capture inspected: colour blends better with the ground, but silhouettes and the soft cutaway footprint still need work. The intermittent two-object shutdown warning recurred. Next: preserve low-profile footprint detail during cutaway.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WASTELAND-CUTAWAY-ROADBED / IN_PROGRESS - Four Overpass and Wrecked Highway variants re-exported with low-profile textured roadbeds independent of the tall ruin occluders. Three export tests passed, including independent root nodes. Rendered Overpass 03 combat reached round 2 and the inspected capture shows a defined roadbed where the blurred footprint was exposed. The roadbed remains simple. Next: refine its breakup and return to other families.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WASTELAND-ROADBED-JOINTS / DONE for fractured cutaway surface. Rebuilt the four Overpass and Wrecked Highway variants on E: with inset concrete fragments and dark seams within the original footprints. Three export tests passed, including two roadbed material surfaces and independent scene roots. Native Overpass 03 skirmish reached round 2. The joins read subtly at the wide zoom. Intermittent ObjectDB shutdown warning recurred. Next: switch to a different map family.

- EXPERIMENTAL-URBAN-LATERAL-FACADES / DONE for the first facade pass. Added lateral windows and upper trim to eligible commercial buildings on six Billboard Avenue and Neon Market scenes. Rebuilt Blender/GLB on E:. Ten targeted tests passed, including visibility grouping and stall integrity. Billboard Avenue rendered and reached round 2. Capture review shows the inward flanks now have detail and outer elevations remain plain. Intermittent ObjectDB warning recurred. Next: address the blank exterior elevations and rooftop relief.

- EXPERIMENTAL-URBAN-REAR-FACADES / DONE for service elevations. Re-exported six urban variants on E: with bilateral window detailing and rear service entrances on eligible blocks. Ten targeted export tests passed. Billboard Avenue capture reviewed and rendered skirmish reached round 2; intermittent shutdown warning recurred. Next: replace remaining flat rooftop features with geometry.

- EXPERIMENTAL-URBAN-ROOF-PARAPETS / DONE for roof edges. Re-exported the six urban variants on E: with four-sided low parapets inside eligible commercial roofs. Six GLB tests passed and the Billboard Avenue native skirmish reached round 2 (217 meshes). Roof equipment baked into the original art still needs individual source-aligned replacements. Next: survey and replace those details in a batch.

- EXPERIMENTAL-BILLBOARD-ROOF-HVAC / IN_PROGRESS - Replaced three painted HVAC footprints on original Billboard Avenue with shared 3D fan units and fitted curbs that cover the underlying art. Re-exported on E:. Six export checks passed, including counts and grouping; native Godot skirmish reached round 2. Next: survey the alternate Billboard texture and apply measured equipment placements where appropriate.

- EXPERIMENTAL-BILLBOARD-VARIANT-HVAC / DONE for the measured fan clusters. Street 15 and Street 16 share the source art and building survey. Added three measured fan clusters to each, reused the same fitted-curb pipeline and rebuilt both GLBs on E:. Six export tests passed. Street 15 reached round 2 in Godot before the final curb inset adjustment; final GLBs passed export checks. Next: review additional rooftop asset families and prioritize replacing painted details of tactical scale.

- EXPERIMENTAL-MARKET-VARIANT-HVAC / DONE for the surveyed clusters. Re-exported Street 02, 07 and 08 Neon Market Alley with respectively two, three and three roof HVAC clusters. Street 07 and 08 share the source image and relevant survey. Ten GLB tests passed, including retained independent stall roots. Street 07 rendered in Godot and reached round 2. Next: address other large flat urban props; remaining roof artwork retains some painted detail.

- EXPERIMENTAL-URBAN-BENCHES / DONE for segmented street seating. Rebuilt the six relevant city scenes on E: with 27 benches aligned to their survey axes, separate seat and back panels and framed supports. Seven export tests passed; the rendered Corporate Plaza skirmish reached round 2. Next: improve the remaining uniform planter and kiosk props while preserving surveyed footprints.

- EXPERIMENTAL-URBAN-PLANTERS / DONE for rebuilt rectangular planters. Re-exported six civic and commercial variants on E: using irregular two-tone foliage, with one mesh per surveyed planter instead of three spheres. Eight targeted export checks passed. The rendered Corporate Plaza skirmish reached round 2 and its environment mesh count fell from 241 to 225. Polygonal planters retain existing flat treatment. Next: rework the civic kiosk blocks.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CIVIC-TERMINALS / DONE for surveyed civic props. Re-exported eleven urban scenes on E: with directionally aligned terminals, screen frames, hoods and kiosk control shelves and vents under the original occlusion groups. Nine export tests passed. Native Civic Center combat reached round 2. Next: inspect finished city and transport capture sets for the highest-impact remaining gaps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TRAIN-TUNNEL-SLEEPERS / DONE for track relief. Rebuilt the experimental Train Tunnel with four edge-to-edge raised rails and two grouped sleeper beds, omitting lower sleepers under the two original maintenance walkways. Two targeted export tests passed. Rendered Godot combat loaded the updated map and reached round 2. Next: review the remaining transit platform props and wide-angle captures.

- EXPERIMENTAL-METRO-INTERCHANGE-COLUMNS / DONE for platform pillar details. Rebuilt Metro Interchange on E: with inset panels, cyan station markers and louver geometry on ten surveyed pillars. Three targeted export checks passed. Godot loaded 367 meshes and the isolated skirmish reached round 2. Next: improve the appearance of the existing stair and platform furniture groups.

- EXPERIMENTAL-METRO-INTERCHANGE-STAIRS / DONE for staircase legibility. Re-exported four surveyed groups on E: with eight amber tread noses and two steel rails apiece. Three targeted export tests passed; native Metro skirmish reached round 2. The intermittent ObjectDB warning reappeared at exit. Next: inspect station furniture and representative captures before further geometry changes.

- EXPERIMENTAL-METRO-TRACK-CONSOLIDATION / DONE for Metro Interchange track groups. Applied sleeper bevels and merged sleepers per surveyed segment, preserving independent ballast/rail meshes and all six existing occlusion roots. Re-exported on E:. Godot environment count fell from 423 to 268 and combat reached round 2. Five targeted tests and two subtests passed, including the distinct Subway Platform rail-exit tests. Frame-time impact unmeasured. Next: use comparative captures to prioritize remaining map detail.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SIX-MAP-COMPARISON / DONE for this visual deliverable. Assembled and inspected E:/CyberCity2085-3d-lab/generated/phase2/urban-transit-wasteland-comparison.png from six originals and real Godot combat captures. The strongest remaining mismatch in this sample is large light-coloured structures versus the dark source architecture, especially the Metro and Overpass. Source images are overhead and combat captures are isometric with HUD. No new runtime tests for the montage. Next: match Metro track and platform materials to the source palette.

- EXPERIMENTAL-METRO-PALETTE / DONE for track and steel palette. Re-exported Metro Interchange with carbon-dark sleepers and ballast and darker matte service steel. Three focused tests passed and native combat reached round 2. Inspected E:/CyberCity2085-3d-lab/generated/phase2/metro-interchange-palette-comparison.png: track bands and walls recede while amber stair edges and cyan fixtures remain legible. Cyan fixtures are still brighter than the source. Next: improve the collapsed overpass palette and large shapes.

- EXPERIMENTAL-OVERPASS-GUARDRAILS / DONE for both variants. Rebuilt Collapsed Overpass 03 and 08 with darker, less saturated concrete and sloped guardrail meshes inside the surveyed widths. Three GLB checks passed, including the four original guardrail visibility groups on 03. Rendered combat reached round 2; intermittent ObjectDB warning recurred. Next: refresh the comparative images and address the largest remaining transport and wasteland shapes.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-FRAME-TIME / DONE for a reproducible three-map sample. Run python experiments/2d_to_3d/run_tactical_preview.py --map metro --benchmark (substitute a catalogue ID for other maps). Uncapped Godot Compatibility camera orbit at 1920x1061 on RTX 5070 Ti, 180 samples after warming up: Metro median/p95 1.50/1.60 ms; Neon Market 3.41/4.28 ms; Collapsed Overpass 1.13/1.24 ms. Twelve Python tests and 134 subtests passed. These are presentation frame intervals during an orbit, with initial loading and combat action playback outside the sample. Next: profile action-time stalls and continue transport/wasteland visual work.

- EXPERIMENTAL-ACTION-REPLY-LATENCY / DONE for mailbox polling. Reduced the active local combat poll interval from 30 to 10 ms and added smoke-only timestamps from successful command publication to reply parsing, before animation playback. Matched Metro runs both reached round 2; six replies had medians 38.9 vs 19.4 ms. The first move remained about 57 ms in both runs. Two runs are a spot comparison, and playback time and CPU overhead were outside the measurements. Next: inspect first-move stalls and resume map-art fixes.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FIRST-MOVE-TIMING / DONE for startup diagnosis. Added smoke-only Python resolution and publication timings. On Metro the initial command measured 58.83 ms end to end (1.07 ms resolve, 3.16 ms publish). Waiting three Godot frames before synthetic input measured 15.61 ms (0.74 ms resolve, 2.52 ms publish). This checks warmed input timing; normal gameplay behavior is unchanged. Both runs reached round 2, with the known shutdown warning on the warm run. Next: return to visual defects and track shutdown diagnostics separately.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-AUDIO-SHUTDOWN / DONE for the observed WAV retention. Verbose Godot reproduced the warning and named AudioStreamWAV plus AudioStreamPlaybackWAV. Finished players now release their streams explicitly, and Escape and smoke completion stop active audio and allow the mixer to drain before quitting. Metro, Neon Market and Overpass completed smoke runs without the warning, plus a final Metro run after adding enemy playback cancellation guards. Other exit routes were not exercised. Next: return to remaining map-art priorities.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TRAIN-TUNNEL-CROSSINGS / DONE for two maintenance crossings. Added low-profile decks, steel curbs and raised grips over source-aligned lower-track crossings while retaining the continuous rails. Rebuilt on E:. Three targeted export tests passed including deck elevation above rails. Native Godot combat reached round 2. Next: widen the visual pass to transit platforms and older maps with large flat objects.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-WINDOW-CLOSE / DONE for native close routing. The experimental battlefield now handles the window close request through the same audio drain as Escape. Godot Metro smoke reached round 2, dispatched the window-close notification and asserted shutdown started; no ObjectDB warning in the run. No physical desktop input used. Next: resume the remaining environment and playability gaps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-ACTOR-IMPORT / DONE for duplicate import elimination. Each battlefield imports each base actor GLB once and instantiates independent actor nodes from a local PackedScene cache. Longshot and Oracle share their source import but retain separate AnimationPlayers and per-instance appearance overrides. Native Metro smoke asserted two base imports for three actors and separate allied animation players, then exercised combat through round 2. Load-time gain was not measured. Next: continue visual and gameplay gaps by map family.

- EXPERIMENTAL-LEGAL-MOVE-MARKERS / DONE for adjacent move previews. Python publishes legal single-step cells per active ally using the terrain and occupancy rule and cover restrictions, and Godot shows up to four reusable markers. Twelve tests and 134 subtests passed, including movement options changing after a move and excluding a blocked cell. Metro rendered skirmish reached round 2. Longer routes are outside this marker pass. Next: surface combat cover and sight feedback and continue art by family.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-COVER-MARKERS / DONE for adjacent cover feedback. Legal movement markers with nearby cover now use amber and the HUD explains the meaning. The Python snapshot applies the engine cover proximity helper without changing the operative position. Twelve tests and 134 subtests passed; native Metro skirmish reached round 2. Facing-specific protection and firing line previews remain open. Next: add target feedback through existing combat rules.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-TARGET-PREVIEW / DONE for hover feedback. Python publishes range, LOS, AP sufficiency and hit chance from the same attack context used by resolution. Godot shows the result when hovering a hostile in the current fire, melee or PSI mode. Twelve tests and 134 subtests passed, including a matching preview and blocked warehouse fire. Native Metro smoke reached round 2; physical pointer interaction not exercised. Next: resume remaining map-art and tactical presentation gaps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-AIM-GUIDE / DONE for visual target preview. A reusable 3D guide appears for valid hovered targets and clears when issuing an action or losing the target. Native Metro smoke projected the hostile into screen coordinates, asserted both the hit chance label and guide visibility, fired and reached round 2. Physical mouse input was not used. Next: resume the remaining tactical map and art gaps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-RESULTS / IN_PROGRESS - Added a Godot end-of-skirmish panel fed by the Python outcome, with surviving-agent count and graceful close action. Terminal snapshots clear legal actions. Thirteen Python tests and 134 subtests passed, including a lethal shot reporting victory and blocking further turns; the action-list assertion was rerun separately. Metro native smoke reached round 2 and loaded the revised HUD, without a terminal result in that scene run. Next: exercise the terminal panel in a native combat and improve remaining map art.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-VICTORY-NATIVE / DONE for the victory path. The opt-in --smoke-victory Warehouse drill uses the real combat engine with deterministic test stats. Godot moved, fired, displayed Victory and 2/2 agents, saved and reviewed E:/CyberCity2085-3d-lab/generated/phase2/warehouse333/tactical_victory.png, and used the panel close action. Python verified sequence 2 and victory; targeted lethal-action test passed. Native defeat remains open. Next: verify defeat and return to map-art priorities.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SKIRMISH-DEFEAT-NATIVE / DONE for both terminal UI paths. The opt-in --smoke-defeat Metro drill uses the real engine with deterministic test stats. Two enemy attacks eliminated both agents, the panel displayed Defeat and 0/2, and its close action completed. Saved E:/CyberCity2085-3d-lab/generated/phase2/interiors/city_transit_metro_interchange/tactical_defeat.png. Re-ran native Warehouse victory against the shared outcome check successfully. Next: return to map art and validate additional map families.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WAREHOUSE-SHELF-STOCK / DONE for the ten racks. Re-exported Warehouse on E: with two fitted cargo objects per shelf tier, using the existing crate model, and retained each rack as an independent occlusion group. Four targeted GLB tests passed. The rendered Warehouse drill reached victory and saved a new capture. Forklifts and loading-bay detail remain open. Next: reconstruct the four surveyed forklifts using the shared industrial model.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WAREHOUSE-FORKLIFTS / DONE for surveyed vehicles. Extracted the shared industrial forklift builder and added wheels. Built four independently occludable units in Warehouse and regenerated the experimental floor mask using the existing wall, rack, crate and container IDs plus the four forklift IDs. Four GLB tests passed, including two forks and four wheels per vehicle. Native Warehouse victory drill passed after re-export. Office, stair and loading-bay art remain open.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WAREHOUSE-EAST-SERVICE / DONE for the three surveyed east-side groups. Added a partitioned office desk and console, eight stair treads with nosing, and a loading deck with cargo. Regenerated the experimental floor mask on E: for the matching original footprints. Four export tests passed, including independent groups; Warehouse native combat reached round 2. Full Warehouse artistic acceptance remains open. Next: visually inspect service groups close up and move to the next industrial environment.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WAREHOUSE-OFFICE-FLOOR / DONE for the visible blur. Native east-side close-up exposed a soft inpainted patch below the office. Added a textured low steel floor confined to its survey and independent of the office cutaway group. Four export tests passed. Inspected the revised E:/CyberCity2085-3d-lab/generated/phase2/warehouse333/east_service_review.png. The office floor is now defined; desk and loading deck materials remain simple. Next: inspect another industrial family and investigate source-row alignment for playability.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TACTICAL-ROW-ALIGNMENT / DONE for the experimental geometry adapter. Found a one-row discrepancy between the legacy bottom-up terrain sampler and the Godot presentation of top-left survey cells. Experimental setup now maps authored rows with height - 1 - row for collision and sight blockers, and shifts curated Y coordinates to preserve their intended survey cells. Production combat and source maps are unchanged. Thirteen tests and 134 subtests passed, including exact surveyed versus presented blocked-cell sets on all 122 maps. Native Warehouse victory, Metro defeat and Skybridge round-2 drills passed. Next: compare more rendered map families and continue the visual backlog.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SUBSTATION-FIXTURES / DONE for surveyed placement. Rebuilt Power Substation on E: with 18 additional groups covering consoles, cabinets, reservoirs and four conduit runs. All original surveyed IDs now have independently occludable GLB groups. Five export tests passed and rendered combat reached round 2. Inspected tactical_cast.png: control screens are too bright and some transformer footprint repair remains visible. Next: refine these materials and cover soft patches with fitted pads.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SUBSTATION-PADS / DONE for transformer floor patches and console intensity. Six fitted textured steel pads stay visible during equipment cutaways. Telemetry screens are dimmer and framed, with separate control buttons. Five export tests passed, native combat reached round 2, and the updated tactical capture was inspected. Smaller switchgear footprints can still show soft floor patches. Next: use the same footprint review on other older industrial maps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FACTORY-CONSOLES-SHELVES / DONE for the five missing surveyed groups. Rebuilt three consoles and two stocked shelves, and patched only their fitted footprints into the retained experimental Factory Floor texture on E:. Six export tests passed and native Godot combat reached round 2. Full artistic acceptance remains open. Next: compare the updated industrial maps and resolve remaining large visual mismatches.

Extended its generator with all eight surveyed forklifts and ten previously flat crate groups, and regenerated the matching floor mask and GLB on E:. Seven targeted export tests passed; native Dockside combat reached round 2. Next: reconstruct the remaining office partitions, machinery and south cargo groups.

Added the final 30 groups including office partitions and controls, machinery, racks, cabins and five south containers, then regenerated the experimental footprint mask on E:. Eight targeted tests passed, asserting all 65 obstacle groups. Dockside skirmish reached round 2 and the capture was inspected. Vessels remain part of the source art and cabin styling stays coarse. Next: assess other maps with major missing surveyed groups.

- EXPERIMENTAL-HOLO-SQUARE / DONE for cutaway groups, benches and landmark. Converted all 39 surveyed obstacles to independently occludable groups, detailed the 14 benches, and modeled the landmark emitter and projection. Ten targeted export tests passed and the native Godot skirmish reached round 2. Full artistic acceptance remains open. Next: review Blacksite and Medical Clinic against their surveys.

- EXPERIMENTAL-BLACKSITE-CLINIC-SURVEY / DONE for surveyed fixture coverage. Routed both showcase scenes through the shared interior builder. Artistic review of the shared furniture, chamber and floor repairs remains open. The before and after native captures showed that bare gray partitions now use the existing worn steel PBR material with face UVs. The clinic reception is visibly lower than adjacent dividers. Eight targeted tests passed, including textured GLB partition primitives, and both Godot skirmishes reached round 2. Next: replace the generic central pod cluster with a fitted Blacksite chamber to remove the visible soft patch, and replace the clinic's industrial scanner placeholder.

- EXPERIMENTAL-BLACKSITE-CLINIC-FIXTURES / DONE for the two landmark replacements. Blacksite has a fitted containment deck covering the soft center patch, with specimen tray, drives and clamps. Medical Clinic has a scanner gantry and patient table. The additions use the existing survey footprints and occlusion groups. Compared the new Godot captures against the prior renders and original map crops. Eight export tests passed, and both skirmishes emitted round-2 success markers. The clinic run also emitted an SDL joystick calibration warning. Secondary worktops and cabinets are still generic. Next: rank other survey gaps and group the next conversions by shared assets.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TECH-MARKET-FACADES / IN_PROGRESS - Read the 122 experimental GLBs against their surveys. Tech Market had the largest gap confirmed in its generator. Added six traced building shells with original roof textures and shared facade windows, plus four additional stalls, for 14 independently grouped fixtures out of 30 surveyed. Rebuilt the experimental floor mask and GLB on E:. Five targeted tests passed and the native Godot run reached round 2. The capture shows the new building volumes and also shows flat irregular stalls and soft patches around some fitted stalls. Next: model irregular canopies and central market fixtures using their original polygons.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TECH-MARKET-SURVEY / DONE for all 30 surveyed groups. Added polygon-fitted raised canopies with original roof artwork, posts, edge rails and stall counters, plus bench, crates and drums, then regenerated the experimental floor and GLB on E:. Next: use the survey audit to reconstruct missing street furniture in the Apartment Block and Civic Center.

- EXPERIMENTAL-APARTMENT-STREET-FIXTURES / DONE for twenty new street groups. Added fitted vehicles, crates, planted beds and trees around the existing buildings. The foliage is stylized and northwest_crate overlaps the northwestern building trace, so it was left for roof-art review. Next: resolve that overlapping roof trace and compare Civic Center's street furniture with its survey.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CIVIC-AND-APARTMENT-TRACES / DONE for full Civic Center survey groups and northwest rooftop cargo. Apartment Block's last trace was identified as rooftop cargo, modeled under its building cutaway and removed from the roof texture. Rebuilt the floor masks and GLBs on E: for Civic Plaza 05, 10 and 11, adding enclosed polygon beds and foliage for their 26 traced islands. - EXPERIMENTAL-SECURITY-CHECKPOINT-BAYS / DONE for surveyed service bays and guardrooms.
