The experimental floor mask and deck pans replace the flat bay artwork. All 29 survey IDs now export as cutaway groups. Nine targeted tests passed, the native skirmish reached round 2, and the new capture was inspected. Floors and furnishings are uniform, and the central barriers still resemble solid cyan beams. Next: rebuild those barriers and scanners with differentiated shapes and details.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SECURITY-CHECKPOINT-FIXTURES / DONE for four barriers, four scanners and six reader terminals. Replaced the bright beams and generic blocks with anchored barrier segments, scanner arches and slatted conveyors, and separate reader/warning panels. Nine targeted export tests passed. Godot reached round 2 and the revised tactical capture was inspected. Bay floor pans remain visually uniform. Next: reconstruct surveyed fixtures still absent in the warehouse and service-tunnel variants.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WAREHOUSE-TUNNEL-FIXTURES / DONE for the twelve identified survey gaps. Exported four fitted workbenches, two drums under one group, and an eight-tread staircase in Warehouse Floor 13. Rebuilt six Service Tunnel groups covering a fan, equipment, terminals and a handwheel valve. Both floor masks were rebuilt on E:. Ten targeted tests passed, both native skirmishes reached round 2 and both captures were inspected. Some painted machinery and uniform partitions remain visible in the tunnel. Next: compare Safehouse and Riot Square traces against their generated scenes and resolve the largest remaining real geometry gaps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SAFEHOUSE-RIOT-SURVEYS / DONE for the identified geometry gaps. Added six Safehouse groups for gym equipment, workbench, generator, washbasin, showers and storage. Rebuilt all 28 surveyed Riot Square groups, including polygon benches, the statue planter with a stylized landmark, and the four perimeter building groups. Both experimental floor masks and GLBs were rebuilt on E:. Twenty-five targeted tests passed, the native captures were reviewed and both skirmishes reached round 2. Riot Square walls and the central monument need further artistic detail. Next: compare elevated freeway rails and reconcile roof/void tags against the active mesh exports.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FREEWAY-RAILS / DONE for the ten surveyed rail lines in Elevated Freeway 08 and 09. Both maps now have individually occludable steel guardrails with upright posts and amber reflectors. The GLB tests check segment counts and world-space coordinates against the surveys. Nineteen targeted tests passed, including existing rooftop deck/void checks. Both native skirmishes reached round 2 and the 09 capture was inspected. The painted interchange still needs a dedicated height and support reconstruction. Next: reconcile surveyed rooftop tags against the exported deck/void geometry, then target the remaining interchange structures that can be reconstructed without changing tactical cells.

- EXPERIMENTAL-ROOFTOP-PARAPETS / DONE for four traced parapets and the deck/void export checks. Rebuilt Rooftop Access and Skybridge Spine on E:. Rooftop Access edges now have capped wall segments and independent cutaway groups. Checked both actual GLBs for deck and street at distinct heights and retaining walls spanning them; source geometry tests confirm surveyed void footprints. Twenty-one targeted tests passed and both Godot skirmishes reached round 2. Next: reconstruct the elevated freeway deck while keeping the experimental tactical grid and walking plane aligned.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FREEWAY-MAIN-DECK / DONE for the straight main span on Elevated Freeway 08 and 09. Rebuilt the two GLBs on E: with a structural slab, side girders and sixteen support piers per map. Only the already blocked rail strips are recessed, preserving the tactical walking height. Seventeen export tests passed, including the tactical cell check, and both Godot skirmishes reached round 2. Curved feeder ramps still use the prior ground presentation. Next: trace the curved ramp decks and reconcile their junctions with the main span within the original play grid.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FREEWAY-CURVED-RAMPS / DONE for the two surveyed curved feeders in each 08/09 map. Used the existing outer traces and inner edges measured from the art, with the ramp surfaces reusing the pavement texture and meeting the main span at its surveyed boundaries. The GLBs include the fascia and preserve the walking plane. Seventeen tests passed and both native runs reached round 2. Reviewed the 09 capture. The surrounding pavement still sits at the existing tactical level, which hides sections of the ramp fascia, and other painted approaches remain open. Next: inventory genuinely unmodeled survey objects across the active catalog before choosing the next shared map batch.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SERVER-VAULT-COOLING / DONE for ten previously missing survey groups in Server Room 05 and Data Vault 15. Reused the fan, generator and storage meshes and assembled the vault control console. Both masks and GLBs were rebuilt on E:. Ten targeted tests passed and both native skirmishes reached round 2. Reviewed the Server Room capture; the existing open shelving still reads as warehouse racks. Next: update their server faces and power indicators or apply the shared medical and plumbing assets to the remaining residential and clinic variants.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SERVER-BANKS / DONE for 31 cabinet groups across the two Server Rooms and Data Vault 15. Replaced open rack silhouettes with fitted graphite cabinets, service blades and status LEDs exported as one mesh per traced group. Eleven targeted tests passed and all three native skirmishes reached round 2. Reviewed the new Server Room 05 capture. General-purpose shelves and older console colors remain visible. Next: reconstruct the missing plumbing and fixtures in the remaining clinic, residential and shelter variants.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CLINIC-SHELTER-PLUMBING / DONE for eight map variants. Added 21 surveyed plumbing and stove groups, plus the storage racks and furnishings selected by the shared interior builder. Rebuilt the experimental masks and GLBs on E:. Twelve targeted tests passed, including checks on all eight exports. Three representative native skirmishes reached round 2. The five other variants were checked at export level. Next: close remaining survey gaps in Corporate Lobby, maintenance depots and bunker command posts.

- EXPERIMENTAL-LOBBY-DEPOT-BUNKER-FIXTURES / DONE for sixteen surveyed groups across five maps. Built paired conference areas and utility cabinets, modelled the depot inspection-pit rims and access rungs and stacked tires, and restored the bunker map desks and controls. Rebuilt the experimental floor masks and exports on E:. Twelve tests passed; one native skirmish per family reached round 2. The inspection pits have a shallow inset above the base floor, and two maps were verified only at export level. Next: rerun the catalog survey comparison and resolve remaining residential and command-center discrepancies.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RESIDENTIAL-COMMAND-DETAILS / DONE for 14 survey discrepancies across Apartment Block 09, two Control Centers, Cyber Cafe and Blacksite Lab 06. Added segmented command monitors and cafe privacy stalls, and reused the fitted seats, bath fixtures and storage. Rebuilt the five experimental GLBs and floor masks on E:. Twelve tests passed and three representative Godot skirmishes reached round 2. The other two maps were checked at export level. Next: distinguish Penthouse Atrium voids from real fixture gaps and finish Maintenance Tunnel and Detention Block equipment.

- EXPERIMENTAL-ATRIUM-TUNNEL-DETENTION / DONE for the visible atrium recess and eight surveyed fixture groups across four maps. Atrium 10 now has a triangulated cutout with shaft sides and a lower surface. Reused plants, baths, seating, a fan, racks and a workbench on the four maps. Thirteen export tests passed and three native skirmishes reached round 2. The Atrium 10 capture confirms the visible recess. The base atrium variant was checked only at export level. Next: triage the remaining individual survey discrepancies, especially the retained research lab consoles.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RESEARCH-LAB-SURVEY-IDENTITY / DONE. Found that the three apparent gaps were upgraded models already present under newer names. Retained the original survey IDs on their cutaway groups and recorded the upgraded assets as GLB metadata, without duplicating geometry. Two targeted tests passed and Research Lab reached round 2 in Godot. Read all 122 active exports. Eight names remain unmatched, including the known modeled rooftop crate and recessed atrium. Next: inspect Neon Market west awning and the command, police, cafe and looted-vault fixtures before resolving those remaining mismatches.

- EXPERIMENTAL-LAST-NAMED-FIXTURE-GAPS / DONE for six identified props. Reconstructed the Neon Market west awning and patched its terrain footprint, added a display to Command Deck, a console to Police Records, the Cyber Cafe 11 counter and the utility cabinets in both Looted Vault variants. Nineteen targeted tests passed and four representative native skirmishes reached round 2. Two interior maps were verified at export level. Next: rerun the catalog-wide fixture audit with the known atrium and rooftop representations, then prioritize visible graphical shortfalls rather than name mismatches.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CATALOG-FIXTURE-AUDIT / DONE for identification coverage. Read 122 active GLBs and matched all 3,517 eligible collision survey IDs, accounting for the existing rooftop crate and true atrium recess. This verifies representation by name, not artistic quality. Rebuilt textured partition faces on Safehouse, Cyber Cafe, Server Room 05, Penthouse 10 and Warehouse 13. Fifteen targeted tests passed and native Safehouse and Penthouse combats reached round 2. Next: target recurring visual defects in the remaining interiors, particularly Service Tunnel wall finish and repeated plain surfaces.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SERVICE-TUNNEL-WALL-FINISH / DONE for 19 traced partitions. Reused the textured steel material even on maps without rectangular wall modules by importing the material source and discarding its temporary scene objects. Rebuilt the Service Tunnel GLB on E:. Fourteen targeted tests passed and native combat reached round 2. Next: address the remaining flat painted machinery and repeated bright console surfaces in the tunnel and shared interior builder.

- EXPERIMENTAL-INTERIOR-CONSOLE-FINISH / DONE for the shared table and terminal finish applied to Service Tunnel, Safehouse and Server Room 05. Added brushed metal worktops, separate dimmer screen surfaces, bezels and input keys. Rebuilt three GLBs, passed fifteen export tests, and reviewed the new Service Tunnel capture after its round-2 native smoke. The two other maps were checked at export level. Remaining tunnel machinery drawn in the original floor art needs explicit visual-only footprints. Next: measure these positions and replace the flat artwork with fitted reused machinery.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SERVICE-TUNNEL-DECOR / DONE for three surveyed decorative vents outside the tactical obstacle register. Reused the twin-fan mesh with low mounting pans, patched only their source rectangles in the experimental floor, and exported each as its own cutaway group. Sixteen targeted tests passed and Godot reached round 2. Next: review the remaining high-impact flat vent and floor details by family rather than repeating the catalog-wide obstacle audit.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SERVICE-TUNNEL-GRATES / DONE for the three surveyed floor grates. Replaced their flat artwork with shallow drain frames and ten bars each. The production floor-detail metadata and walkability are preserved. Sixteen targeted tests passed and the native skirmish reached round 2. Next: compare the Dockside floor details and prioritize contrast and lighting issues across the map families.

- EXPERIMENTAL-DOCKSIDE-FLOOR-GRATE / DONE. Reused the shallow Service Tunnel drain builder for Dockside's east-center grate, updated the experimental floor mask, and exported the extra cutaway group on E:. Sixteen targeted tests passed and Dockside combat reached round 2. Next: review lighting and contrast across representative indoor and outdoor map families, then apply common improvements in batches.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TACTICAL-ACTOR-FILL / DONE for the shared experimental renderer. Added a shadow-free cool fill light on an actor-only render layer, retaining the existing map lights and shadows. Native Service Tunnel and Dockside skirmishes both reached round 2. Compared before/after close-ups on E:; the tunnel capture shows a subtle improvement on dark clothing and equipment. The full catalog has not been reviewed visually under the shared light. Next: examine lighting and scene costs across representative exterior and interior tactical zooms.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TACTICAL-CUTAWAY-CACHE / DONE for stationary views. The shared cutaway reuses intersection results until a camera or target ray changes and continues timed reveals after motion stops. The headless regression passed for blocked, stationary reveal and camera invalidation. Native Apartment Block and Service Tunnel skirmishes reached round 2. Frame-rate impact has not been measured. Next: measure the existing renderer benchmark on contrasting scenes before selecting another optimization.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TACTICAL-BENCHMARK-VIEWS / DONE for three rendered camera conditions. The benchmark now reports a wide orbit, stationary view at tactical zoom and orbit at tactical zoom in the actual Godot renderer, 180 frames per view. At 1920x1061 uncapped with RTX 5070 Ti, p95 was 1.82/1.60/1.85 ms in Service Tunnel and 2.35/2.06/2.40 ms in Apartment Block. The repeated wide measurements agreed closely with prior baselines. This measures idle units and camera views only. Next: benchmark active action/animation sequences and dense maps to check for spikes that isolated orbits miss.

- EXPERIMENTAL-ACTIVE-COMBAT-BENCHMARK / DONE for the scripted three-unit combat. Added --benchmark-combat to run real Python/Godot movement, fire, enemy phase, aid, defend and PSI without PNG capture overhead. At 1920x1061 uncapped, Service Tunnel measured 562 frames, p95 1.89 ms and max 11.38 ms. Tech Market measured 761 frames, p95 2.63 ms and max 15.71 ms. Both emitted round-2 success and completed PSI animation. PowerShell logged the existing SDL joystick-calibration error. Next: measure larger squads and additional active effects, or prioritize the visible art gaps where these short runs show no sustained rendering bottleneck.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TACTICAL-HUD-LEGIBILITY / DONE for the shared tactical scene. Separated the compact top-left unit and action readout from bottom-anchored controls, preserved the current attack-mode hint, and added outline contrast. Tech Market native combat reached round 2. Reviewed its 1920x1061 capture and confirmed that the smaller HUD leaves the central map clear. Narrow viewport and controller layouts remain unverified. Next: address Tech Market's visibly soft exposed floor below open canopies.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TECH-MARKET-PAVEMENT / DONE for the masked surface detail pass. Added a high-frequency concrete texture to the repaired footprints while preserving their underlying lighting and leaving all pixels outside the mask unchanged. Rebuilt floor and GLB on E:, passed seven tests and native Tech Market reached round 2. The capture still shows broadly soft transitions in some areas under the canopies. Next: replace the worst areas with matched pavement donor patches, and reserve this fine-detail pass for subtle cleanup.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TECH-MARKET-DONOR-PAVEMENT / DONE for five large canopy footprints. Replaced broad interpolation with blended pavement sampled from adjacent aisles and confined each patch to its traced polygon. Eight targeted tests passed, the native skirmish reached round 2, and the capture shows clearer ground under the large central and southeast canopies. Some smaller peripheral patches remain soft. Next: add depth and facade variation to the market perimeter buildings before moving the same floor approach to other maps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-TECH-MARKET-FACADES-DETAIL / DONE for six perimeter buildings. Added mapped weathered concrete alongside the retained roof artwork and fitted inward-facing shop entrances with three alternating lamp tints. Eight targeted tests passed and Tech Market reached round 2 in Godot. Next: apply proven facade and pavement treatments to other representative marketplace and plaza maps with similarly plain surfaces.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CIVIC-FACADE-FINISH / DONE for Riot Square, Civic Center and Civic Plaza 05/10/11. Added mapped concrete to their exterior walls, plus window modules and trim bands on Riot Square's irregular footprints. The first native capture showed the concrete was too bright, so the final exports reuse the tinted texture generated by the Tech Market floor preparation. Twenty-two targeted tests passed. Riot Square and Civic Center reached round 2 in the initial native pass, and Riot Square was rerun after tinting. Next: improve remaining landmark silhouettes where the rooftop art still reads flatter than its surroundings.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RIOT-SQUARE-LANDMARK / DONE for the landmark silhouette within the retained survey group. Replaced the small generic beacon with an octagonal stepped plinth and stylized bronze sculpture, keeping vegetation clear of the central base. Twenty-two export tests passed and the native Riot Square skirmish reached round 2. The sculpture is an interpretation of the overhead reference. Next: apply the same reference comparison to the other civic monuments and check camera legibility around the tallest ones.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CIVIC-PLAZA-LANDMARKS / DONE for the four surveyed monuments in variants 05, 10 and 11. Added raised octagonal plinths, collars and framed luminous cores, with a shorter magenta east monument in Plaza 05. Twenty-two tests passed. Native Plaza 05 and 10 skirmishes reached round 2, while Plaza 11 was checked at export level. Next: inspect the remaining flat or generic landmarks beyond the civic family and organize the next shared art pass.

- EXPERIMENTAL-PENTHOUSE-FOUNTAIN / DONE. Replaced the generic cylindrical landmark with a rectangular stepped basin fitted to the original artwork, inset water, submerged lighting and a separate central jet under the original cutaway group. Sixteen targeted tests passed and the native Penthouse Atrium skirmish reached round 2. Water remains static. Next: prioritize another clearly visible color or silhouette discrepancy across the experimental maps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-PENTHOUSE-WATER-RIPPLE / DONE for a subtle looping ripple. Blender exports a single scale animation on the ripple mesh and the experimental Godot importer starts that named animation. Seventeen tests passed, including the animation payload check, and native Penthouse combat reached round 2. The water effect is decorative and its motion has not been reviewed in video. Next: inspect a separate map family for larger visible gaps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-COLLAPSED-OVERPASS-FRACTURES / DONE for the broken deck surfaces. Added inset raised concrete fragments and dark seams on the actual overpass slabs, preserving their surveyed silhouettes and retained roadbeds. Three targeted export tests passed and the Wasteland City 03 native escarmouche reached round 2. This change covers the single collapsed-overpass map. Next: check bold silhouette differences between the remaining Wasteland vehicle models and their originals.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WASTELAND-CONVOY-SILHOUETTES / DONE for five trucks on Convoy Crossing. Replaced five generic wreck models with the surveyed tanker, crate flatbed and three covered trucks. Reused the existing occlusion and terrain footprints. Four targeted tests passed and the native skirmish reached round 2. The newly modeled trucks use simple weathered materials. Next: review other Wasteland wreck silhouettes for distinct reference-specific shapes and retain the SUVs where the reference calls for them.

- EXPERIMENTAL-WRECKED-HIGHWAY-FLATBEDS / DONE for four heavy wrecks per variant in Wrecked Highway 03 and 08. Fitted exposed truck frames to the traced bounds and matched reference yaw while leaving smaller car silhouettes to the existing SUV asset. Six targeted tests passed; variant 03 reached round 2 in Godot and variant 08 was checked at export level. Next: compare Rusted Trainyard wagon silhouettes and side details with the source art.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUSTED-TRAINYARD-WAGONS / DONE for both 04 and 09 variants. Replaced generic containers on the tracks with fitted covered wagons, open gondolas and cylindrical tankers. The original survey and gameplay positions remain the placement source. Eight tests passed; variant 04 reached round 2 in Godot and variant 09 was checked at export level. The track and turntable are still largely represented in painted ground. Next: model the turntable and selected rail segments where their silhouettes affect the showcase.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUSTED-TRAINYARD-TURNTABLE / IN_PROGRESS. Rebuilt variants 04 and 09 with the surveyed diagonal wagon, two raised bridge rails and sleepers in the experimental GLBs on E:. Eight targeted tests passed. Godot variant 04 reached round 2 despite the existing SDL calibration warning. Variant 09 was checked at export level. The circular rim remains painted. Next: give the rim depth and review the silhouette at tactical zoom.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUSTED-TRAINYARD-RIM / DONE for geometry in both 04 and 09 exports. Added a shallow beveled steel rim below the deck rails, sharing the bridge transform. Rebuilt both GLBs on E: and passed eight targeted tests. Variant 04 reached round 2 in Godot despite the SDL calibration warning. Visual review at tactical zoom remains open. Next: compare the exported courtyard and track against the original at combat zoom, then move to the next map family.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUSTED-TRAINYARD-ART-REVIEW / IN_PROGRESS. Generated and inspected the variant 04 Godot overview after the rim export. The diagonal bridge and ring are visible together. The floor under the wagon still has a soft repair patch, and the combat close-up frames the squad rather than the turntable. Updated both catalogue entries to describe their actual geometry. Next: replace the blurred patch with matched ballast and capture the turntable at tactical zoom.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUSTED-TRAINYARD-BALLAST / DONE for the turntable footprint. Blended ballast sampled from the original yard into the wagon repair rectangle on variants 04 and 09, retaining all pixels outside that rectangle in this pass. Rebuilt both GLBs on E:. Seventeen focused tests passed. The refreshed variant 04 Godot overview shows texture where the previous capture had a smooth patch. Variant 09 visual review and a dedicated tactical close-up remain open. Next: review both warehouse roofs against the original map art.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUSTED-TRAINYARD-WORKSHOPS / DONE for silhouettes in 04 and 09. Replaced the solid blocks at the north siding with roofless workshops, broken walls and reused industrial equipment. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, nine tests passed and the 09 Godot overview was inspected. Slabs still look uniform. Next: recover the original slab texture and add original-map details to the larger workshop.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUSTED-TRAINYARD-SLABS / DONE for original ground detail on the raised workshop slabs in 04 and 09. Their top faces use original map UVs, while concrete side materials and existing footprints are preserved. Nine tests passed, both exports were rebuilt on E:, and the 04 Godot overview shows the restored surface detail. Some drawn equipment remains flat. Next: match the larger workshop's equipment to its original placements or address broader family gaps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUSTED-TRAINYARD-FIXTURES / DONE for the selected workshop assets. Placed two service cabinets, a parts crate and a fan assembly at positions sampled from the original art on both variants. Rebuilt both E: exports. Nine tests passed and variant 04 rendered in Godot. Remaining flat details include smaller scrap baked into the slab material. Next: shift the environment pass to a different map family.

- EXPERIMENTAL-COLLAPSED-OVERPASS-08 / DONE for parity with variant 03. Re-exported 08 on E: with raised deck fragments, aggregate, seams and retained roadbed. Ten targeted tests passed, with both variants covered. Godot imported 85 meshes for 08 and the new overview was inspected. The curved ramps remain painted. Next: trace the curved ramps for raised geometry following the illustrated roadbeds.

- EXPERIMENTAL-COLLAPSED-OVERPASS-RAMPS / IN_PROGRESS. Reconstructed four curved road ribbons with textured surfaces and raised edges on variants 03 and 08. Both GLBs were rebuilt on E: and ten targeted tests passed. Godot rendered variant 08. Next: review the ramp junctions against the source and refine the relief without changing tactical elevations.

- EXPERIMENTAL-COLLAPSED-OVERPASS-APPROACHES / DONE for the ramp junction slopes. Tapered the eight approaches toward the original road surface in variants 03 and 08. Both exports rebuilt on E: and ten tests passed. Inspected the refreshed 08 Godot overview. Next: address the repeated bare ruin silhouettes around the ramps.

- EXPERIMENTAL-COLLAPSED-OVERPASS-RUINS / DONE for differentiated silhouettes. Both variants now distinguish broken retaining walls with fallen slabs around the ramps from taller exterior walls with corner piers. Preserved surveyed groups, rebuilt both GLBs on E:, passed ten targeted tests and rendered 08 in Godot. Next: compare materials and remaining flat debris against the source before moving to another family.

- EXPERIMENTAL-COLLAPSED-OVERPASS-DEBRIS / DONE for 32 textured 3D fragments per variant, grouped within the eight existing ruin footprints. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten targeted tests passed and Godot rendered variant 08. Smaller background debris remains in the floor artwork. Next: move to the Ruined Downtown family and inspect its largest flat or generic structures.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUINED-DOWNTOWN-FRAMES / DONE for exposed structural frames on six surveyed ruins per variant. Added open girders, columns and surviving upper-floor fragments to 01 and 06. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten targeted tests passed and Godot rendered 06. Next: differentiate the large wrecks and refine the damaged facades.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUINED-DOWNTOWN-HEAVY-WRECKS / DONE for three surveyed heavy wrecks per variant. Added two angled, partially roofless bus shells and a truck fitted to their original bounds in 01 and 06. Both exports rebuilt on E:, ten targeted tests passed and Godot rendered 06. Next: add original facade color and panel remnants to the ruined blocks.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUINED-DOWNTOWN-CLADDING / DONE for the surveyed street-facing ruins. Added surviving faded blue panels and corroded mounting rails, with gaps based on the orientation of each ruin. Both variants rebuilt on E:, ten targeted tests passed and Godot rendered 06. Next: inspect a different Wasteland family for larger flat structures.

- EXPERIMENTAL-BURNED-MARKET-STALLS / DONE for the stall silhouettes on 02 and 07. Replaced intact display stalls with scorched counters, open frames and torn canvas on selected stalls. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten tests passed and Godot rendered 07. Next: distinguish canopy materials and compare corner ruins to source.

- EXPERIMENTAL-BURNED-MARKET-CANOPIES / DONE for the differentiated covers on 02 and 07. Added rust-red fabric to the north and east stalls and replaced the southeast canvas with a blue corrugated sheet. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten targeted tests passed and Godot rendered 07. Next: model the roofless market corner buildings.

- EXPERIMENTAL-BURNED-MARKET-RUINS / DONE for roofless building silhouettes in variants 02 and 07. Added exposed joists, burned posts, surviving crossbeams and dropped floor fragments within all eight surveyed ruin groups per map. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten tests passed and Godot rendered 07. Next: inspect Flooded Civic Square for water and ruin geometry that still reads flat.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FLOODED-CIVIC-LANDMARKS / DONE for the central monument and six planters in variants 04 and 09. Added octagonal monument tiers and coping along each surveyed planter outline. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten targeted tests passed and the 04 scene rendered in Godot. Next: trace the large water surfaces and retain the original tactical terrain.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FLOODED-CIVIC-WATER / DONE for the traced main plaza surface on both variants. Added a separate shallow mesh using the original repaired pavement texture and reduced roughness. No changes to tactical terrain. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten focused tests passed and 04 rendered in Godot. The water is static and adjacent street puddles remain in the ground texture. Next: compare the perimeter ruin silhouettes and original debris.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FLOODED-CIVIC-RUINS / DONE for eight perimeter buildings per variant. Added blue plinth panels, exposed steel and a tilted upper-floor fragment inside the measured shells in 04 and 09. Both exports rebuilt on E:, ten focused tests passed and variant 09 rendered in Godot. Next: compare the flooded bus with its original silhouette and fit the existing damaged bus model where appropriate.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FLOODED-CIVIC-BUS / DONE for the west-side wreck in variants 04 and 09. Reused the open bus shell, angled and fitted to the surveyed footprint on both maps. Both exports rebuilt on E:, ten focused tests passed and variant 09 rendered in Godot. Next: compare Scavenger Plaza against its original makeshift barriers and structures.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SCAVENGER-PLAZA-BARRICADES / DONE for both traced barricades on 05 and 10. Replaced uniform concrete with scrap metal panels braced along the original polylines. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten targeted tests passed and Godot rendered variant 10. Next: differentiate the salvaged stalls from one another using the source canopy layout.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SCAVENGER-PLAZA-STALLS / DONE for twelve traced stalls per map in variants 05 and 10. Replaced repeated shop instances with open parts counters and single or paired canvas covers fitted to the source color layout. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten tests passed and variant 10 rendered in Godot. Next: differentiate the vehicle hulks and preserve more of the perimeter ruin silhouettes.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SCAVENGER-PLAZA-FLATBED / DONE for the southeast wreck in both variants. Fitted the existing stripped flatbed to the diagonal reference while keeping the edge cars. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten tests passed and Godot rendered 10. Next: reconstruct the perimeter ruin silhouettes.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SCAVENGER-PLAZA-RUINS / DONE for four perimeter groups per variant. Added fractured piers, exposed beams and slumped floor panels within their surveyed footprints. Both exports rebuilt on E:, ten tests passed and Godot rendered 10. Next: inspect Ruined Transit Plaza for distinctive structures that still use generic models.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUINED-TRANSIT-CARRIAGE / DONE for the surveyed west train. Replaced the generic container with a carriage with cab ends, open windows, bogies and roof vents. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, passed ten tests and rendered the scene in Godot. Next: reconstruct the platform edges, stair orientations and blue station cladding.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUINED-TRANSIT-PLATFORMS / DONE for both platforms and stair orientation. Added mirrored platform coping and surviving blue panels, and reversed south stairs to rise away from the central plaza. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, passed ten targeted tests and rendered in Godot. Next: inspect the south carriage and whether it can be modeled without widening the surveyed tactical edge.

- EXPERIMENTAL-RUINED-TRANSIT-SOUTH-CARRIAGE / DONE for the visible wreck beyond the southern boundary. Reused carriage geometry with exposed roof ribs, removed the old painted silhouette in the experimental floor and left tactical geometry intact. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, ten tests passed and the new scene rendered in Godot. Next: inspect Cratered Waterfront for its largest flat landmark.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CRATERED-WATERFRONT-WATER / DONE for the three surveyed water polygons: west basin, crater inlet and east basin. Added textured surfaces with a distinct roughness while preserving the original combat survey. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, ten tests passed and the scene rendered in Godot. Water remains static. Next: differentiate the crater-side broken quay from the intact edges.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CRATERED-WATERFRONT-QUAYS / DONE for the three existing quay polylines. Added continuous coping on east and west and tilted fractured blocks on the crater edge while preserving surveyed line geometry. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, passed ten tests and rendered in Godot. Next: reconstruct the prominent shipwreck silhouettes along the waterfront from the original art.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CRATERED-WATERFRONT-WRECKS / IN_PROGRESS. Reconstructed two prominent basin wrecks as tapered steel hulls with exposed deck frames, following their angles in the original art. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, ten tests passed and the scene rendered in Godot. Painted hull traces still need localized water repair; next: remove those traces and review the result.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CRATERED-WATERFRONT-WRECK-CLEANUP / DONE for the two reconstructed hull footprints. Masked the painted hulls and restored water detail using nearby source samples. Rebuilt the GLB and rendered in Godot. Eighteen tests passed after correcting a stale merged-stall assumption for Burned Market; other market exports retain their merge check. Next: inspect the largest west-edge wreck and compare inland ruins.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CRATERED-WATERFRONT-WEST-HULL / DONE for the large western wreck. Added a tapered cargo hull and raised hatch remnants aligned to the reference, and repaired the painted hull footprint with sampled water. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, passed 18 tests and rendered in Godot. Next: review the inland buildings against the original.

- EXPERIMENTAL-CRATERED-WATERFRONT-WAREHOUSES / DONE for the three surveyed north-side buildings. Added raised steel framing, surviving blue facade fragments and collapsed deck pieces. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, ten targeted tests passed and Godot rendered the scene. Next: inspect the outdoor Wasteland families for large groups still represented by generic vehicles or rocks.

- EXPERIMENTAL-IRRADIATED-BASIN-DRAINS / DONE for both variants. Replaced generic machine meshes with four hollow concrete drains per map, with horizontal outfalls and a vertical southern opening. Rebuilt both GLBs, passed ten focused tests and rendered 05 in Godot. Next: inspect the basin water and southwest tank.

- EXPERIMENTAL-IRRADIATED-BASIN-TANK / DONE for the southwest tank on 05 and 10. Fitted an open corroded shell and three reinforcing bands to the original diagonal footprint. Both GLBs rebuilt on E:, ten targeted tests passed and 10 rendered in Godot. Next: trace basin-water polygons around dry islands and preserve existing tactical movement.

- EXPERIMENTAL-IRRADIATED-BASIN-WATER / DONE for four major pools per variant. Traced surfaces around the raised islands and applied a distinct roughness to the source water artwork. Tactical geometry was preserved. Both exports rebuilt on E:, ten tests passed and Godot rendered 10. Smaller wet channels still use the floor texture. Next: inspect the silhouette of the large wrecks on Salt Flat Ambush.

- EXPERIMENTAL-SALT-FLAT-HEAVY-WRECKS / DONE for two vehicles. Reused the tanker and open flatbed and fit their diagonal silhouettes to the south and east survey footprints. Rebuilt the GLB on E:, passed ten tests and rendered in Godot. Next: compare the low fences and poles with the source.

- EXPERIMENTAL-FLOODED-CIVIC-WET-CONCRETE / DONE. Low-angle review of Waterfront Docks and Flooded Civic Square confirmed water mipmaps and exposed overly warm concrete in the flooded ruins. Rebuilt variants 04 and 09 with a cooler concrete tint and surface normals. Eleven outdoor tests passed and variant 04 rendered from the low camera. Next: compare the revised ruins at combat zoom.

Added broken concrete fragments inside the triangulated original planter footprints and rebuilt both 04/09 exports on E:. Eleven outdoor tests passed and the updated 04 tactical run reached round two. Next: compare exposed ruin interiors and original floor debris at combat scale.

Rebuilt both Flooded Civic Square variants on E:, passed eleven outdoor tests and reached round two on 09. Next: prioritize a different family with larger flat landmark gaps.
